Map<TaskId, Set<TopicPartition>> standbyTasksToCreate,\n458:                                                 final Map<Task, Set<TopicPartition>> tasksToRecycle,\n459:                                                 final Set<Task> tasksToCloseClean) {\n460:         for (final Task task : tasks.allTasks()) {\n461:             final TaskId taskId = task.id();\n462:             if (activeTasksToCreate.containsKey(taskId)) {\n463:                 if (task.isActive()) {\n464:                     final Set<TopicPartition> topicPartitions = activeTasksToCreate.get(taskId);\n465:                     if (tasks.updateActiveTaskInputPartitions(task, topicPartitions)) {\n466:                         task.updateInputPartitions(topicPartitions, topologyMetadata.nodeToSourceTopics(task.id()));\n467:                     }\n468:                     task.resume();\n469:                 } else {\n470:                     tasksToRecycle.put(task, activeTasksToCreate.get(taskId));\n471:                 }\n472:                 activeTasksToCreate.remove(taskId);\n473:             } else if (standbyTasksToCreate.containsKey(taskId)) {\n474:            } else {\n478:                     tasksToRecycle.put(task, standbyTasksToCreate.get(taskId));\n479:                 }\n480:                 standbyTasksToCreate.remove(taskId);\n481:             } else {\n482:                 tasksToCloseClean.add(task);\n483:             }\n484:         }\n485:     }\n486: \n487:     private void updateInputPartitionsOfStandbyTaskIfTheyChanged(final Task task,\n488:                                                                  final Set<TopicPartition> inputPartitions) {\n489:         /*\n490:         We should only update input partitions of a standby task if the input partitions really changed. Updating the\n491:         input partitions of tasks also updates the mapping from source nodes to input topics in the processor topology\n492:         within the task. The mapping is updated with the topics from the topology metadata. The topology metadata does\n493:         not prefix intermediate internal topics with the application ID. Thus, if a standby task has input partitions\n494:         from an intermediate internal topic the update of the mapping in the processor topology leads to an invalid\n495:         topology exception during recycling of a standby task to an active task when the input queues are created. This\n496:         is because the input topics in the processor topology and the input partitions of the task do not match because\n497:         the former miss the application ID prefix.\n498:         For standby task that have only input partitions from intermediate internal topics this check avoids the invalid\n499:         topology exception. Unfortunately, a subtopology might have input partitions subscribed to with a regex\n500:         additionally intermediate internal topics which might still lead to an invalid topology exception during recycling\n501:         irrespectively of this check here. Thus, there is still a bug to fix here.\n502:          */\n503:         if (!task.inputPartitions().equals(inputPartitions)) {\n504:             task.updateInputPartitions(inputPartitions, topologyMetadata.nodeToSourceTopics(task.id()));\n505:         }\n506:     }\n507: \n508:     private void handleTasksWithStateUpdater(final Map<TaskId, Set<TopicPartition>> activeTasksToCreate,\n509:                                              final Map<TaskId, Set<TopicPartition>> standbyTasksToCreate,\n510:                                              final Map<Task, Set<TopicPartition>> tasksToRecycle,\n511:                                              final Set<Task> tasksToCloseClean,\n512:                                              final Map<TaskId, RuntimeException> failedTasks) {\n513:         handleTasksPendingInitialization();\n514:         handleRestoringAndUpdatingTasks(activeTasksToCreate, standbyTasksToCreate, failedTasks);\n515:         handleRunningAndSuspendedTasks(activeTasksToCreate, standbyTasksToCreate, tasksToRecycle, tasksToCloseClean);\n516:     }\n517: \n518:     private void handleTasksPendingInitialization() {\n519:         // All tasks pending initialization are not part of the usual bookkeeping\n520: \n521:         final Set<Task> tasksToCloseDirty = new HashSet<>();\n522: \n523:         for (final Task task :                                  final Map<TaskId, Set<TopicPartition>> standbyTasksToCreate,\n533:                                                 final Map<Task, Set<TopicPartition>> tasksToRecycle,\n534:                                                 final Set<Task> tasksToCloseClean) {\n535:         for (final Task task : tasks.allNonFailedTasks()) {\n536:             if topologyMetadata.nodeToSourceTopics(task.id()));\n557:         }\n558:         if (task.state() == State.SUSPENDED) {\n559:             tasks.removeTask(task);\n560:             task.resume();\n561:             stateUpdater.add(task);\n562:         }\n563:     }\n564: \n565:     private void handleRestoringAndUpdatingTasks(final Map<TaskId, Set<TopicPartition>> activeTasksToCreate,\n566: final Map<Task, Set<TopicPartition>> tasksToRecycle,\n598:                                            final Set<Task> tasksToCloseCleanFromStateUpdater,\n599:                                            final Set<Task> tasksToCloseDirtyFromStateUpdater,\n600:                                            final Map<TaskId, RuntimeException> failedTasks) {\n601:         final Map<TaskId,                final CompletableFuture<StateUpdater.RemovedTaskResult> future = stateUpdater.remove(taskId);\n614:                         futuresForUpdatingInputPartitions.put(taskId, future);\n615: activeInputPartitions.put(taskId, activeTasksToCreate.get(taskId));\n621:                 }\n622:                 activeTasksToCreate.remove(taskId);\n623:             } else if         final Map<TaskId, RuntimeException> failedTasks) {\n644:         getNonFailedTasks(futures, failedTasks).forEach(task -> {\n645:             task.updateInputPartitions(\n646:                 newInputPartitions.get(task.id()),\n647:                 topologyMetadata.nodeToSourceTopics(task.id())\n648:             );\n649:             stateUpdater.add(task);\n650:         });\n651:     }\n652: \n653:     private void addToActiveTasksToRecycle(final Map<TaskId, CompletableFuture<StateUpdater.RemovedTaskResult>> futures,\n654:                                            final        final Map<TaskId, RuntimeException> failedTasks) {\n657:         getNonFailedTasks(futures, failedTasks).forEach(task -> tasksToRecycle.put(task, standbyInputPartitions.get(task.id())));\n658:     }\n659: \n660:     private void addToStandbyTasksToRecycle(final Map<TaskId, CompletableFuture<StateUpdater.RemovedTaskResult>> futures,\n661:                                             final    }\n666: \n667:     private Stream<Task> getNonFailedTasks(final Map<TaskId, CompletableFuture<StateUpdater.RemovedTaskResult>> futures,\n668:                                            final .filter(Objects::nonNull)\n672:             .map(removedTaskResult -> checkIfTaskFailed(removedTaskResult, failedTasks))\n673:             .filter(Objects::nonNull);\n674:     }\n675: \n676: \n677:     private void addToTasksToClose(final Map<TaskId, CompletableFuture<StateUpdater.RemovedTaskResult>> futures,\n678:                                    final Set<Task> (removedTaskResult.exception().isPresent()) {\n685:                     tasksToCloseDirtyFromStateUpdater.add(removedTaskResult.task());\n686:                 } else {\n687:                     tasksToCloseCleanFromStateUpdater.add(removedTaskResult.task());\n688:                 }\n689:             });\n690:     }\n691: \n692:     private Task checkIfTaskFailed(final StateUpdater.RemovedTaskResult removedTaskResult,\n693:                                    final Map<TaskId, RuntimeException> failedTasks) {\n694:         final Task task = removedTaskResult.task();\n695:         if (removedTaskResult.exception().isPresent()) {\n696:             failedTasks.put(task.id(), removedTaskResult.exception().get());\n697:             tasks.addFailedTask(task);\n698:             return null;\n699:         }\n700:         return task;\n701:     }\n702: \n703:     private StateUpdater.RemovedTaskResult waitForFuture(final TaskId taskId,\n704:                                                          final CompletableFuture<StateUpdater.RemovedTaskResult> future) {\n705:         final StateUpdater.RemovedTaskResult removedTaskResult;\n706:         try {\n707:             removedTaskResult = future.get();\n708:             if (removedTaskResult == null) {\n709:                 throw new IllegalStateException(\"Task \" + taskId + \" was not found in the state updater. \"\n710:                     + BUG_ERROR_MESSAGE);\n711:             }\n712:             return removedTaskResult;\n713:         } catch (final ExecutionException executionException) {\n714:             log.warn(\"An exception happened when removing task {} from the state updater. The task was added to the \" +\n715:                     \"failed task in the state updater: \",\n716:                 taskId, executionException);\n717:             return null;\n718:         } catch (final InterruptedException shouldNotHappen) {\n719:             Thread.currentThread().interrupt();\n720:             log.error(INTERRUPTED_ERROR_MESSAGE, shouldNotHappen);\n721:             throw new IllegalStateException(INTERRUPTED_ERROR_MESSAGE, shouldNotHappen);\n722:         }\n723:     }\n724: \n725:     private Map<TaskId, Set<TopicPartition>> pendingTasksToCreate(final Map<TaskId, Set<TopicPartition>> tasksToCreate) {\n726:         final Map<TaskId, Set<TopicPartition>> pendingTasks = new HashMap<>();\n727:         final Iterator<Map.Entry<TaskId, Set<TopicPartition>>> iter = tasksToCreate.entrySet().iterator();\n728:         while (iter.hasNext()) {\n729:             final Map.Entry<TaskId, Set<TopicPartition>> entry = iter.next();\n730:             final TaskId taskId = entry.getKey();\n731:             final boolean taskIsOwned = tasks.allTaskIds().contains(taskId)\n732:                 || (stateUpdater != null && stateUpdater.tasks().stream().anyMatch(task -> task.id() == taskId));\n733:             if (taskId.topologyName() != null && !taskIsOwned && !topologyMetadata.namedTopologiesView().contains(taskId.topologyName())) {\n734:                 log.info(\"Cannot create the assigned task {} since it's topology name cannot be recognized, will put it \" +\n735:                         \"aside as pending for now and create later when topology metadata gets refreshed\", taskId);\n736:                 pendingTasks.put(taskId, entry.getValue());\n737:                 iter.remove();\n738:             }\n739:         }\n740:         return pendingTasks;\n741:     }\n742: \n743:     private Map<TaskId, RuntimeException> closeAndRecycleTasks(final Map<Task, Set<TopicPartition>> tasksToRecycle,\n744:                                                                final Set<Task> tasksToCloseClean) {\n745:         final Map<TaskId, RuntimeException> taskCloseExceptions = new LinkedHashMap<>();\n746:         final Set<Task> tasksToCloseDirty = new TreeSet<>(Comparator.comparing(Task::id));\n747: \n748:         // for all tasks to close or recycle, we should first write a checkpoint as in post-commit\n749:         final List<Task> tasksToCheckpoint = new ArrayList<>(tasksToCloseClean);\n750:         tasksToCheckpoint.addAll(tasksToRecycle.keySet());\n751:         for (final Task task : tasksToCheckpoint) {\n752:             try {\n753:                 // Note that we are not actually committing here but just check if we need to write checkpoint file:\n754:                 // 1) for active tasks prepareCommit should return empty if it has committed during suspension successfully,\n755:                 //    and their changelog positions should not change at all postCommit would not write the checkpoint again.\n756:                 // 2) for standby tasks prepareCommit should always return empty, and then in postCommit we would probably\n757:                 //    write the checkpoint file.\n758:                 final Map<TopicPartition, OffsetAndMetadata> offsets = task.prepareCommit(true);\n759:                 if (!offsets.isEmpty()) {\n760:                     log.error(\"Task {} should have been committed when it was suspended, but it reports non-empty \" +\n761:                                     \"offsets {} to commit; this means it failed during last commit and hence should be closed dirty\",\n762:                             task.id(), offsets);\n763: \n764:                     tasksToCloseDirty.add(task);\n765:                 } else if (!task.isActive()) {\n766:                     // For standby tasks, always try to first suspend before committing (checkpointing) it;\n767:                     // Since standby tasks do not actually need to commit offsets but only need to\n768:                     // flush / checkpoint state stores, so we only need to call postCommit here.\n769:                     task.suspend();\n770: \n771:                     task.postCommit(true);\n772:                 }\n773:             } catch (final RuntimeException e) {\n774:                 final String uncleanMessage = String.format(\n775:                         \"Failed to checkpoint task %s. Attempting to close remaining tasks before re-throwing:\",\n776:                         task.id());\n777:                 log.error(uncleanMessage, e);\n778:                 taskCloseExceptions.putIfAbsent(task.id(), e);\n779:                 // We've already recorded the exception (which is the point of clean).\n780:                 // Now, we should go ahead and complete the close because a half-closed task is no good to anyone.\n781:                 tasksToCloseDirty.add(task);\n782:             }\n783:         }\n784: \n785:         tasksToCloseClean.removeAll(tasksToCloseDirty);\n786:         for (final Task task : tasksToCloseClean) {\n787:             try {\n788:                 closeTaskClean(task);\n789:             } catch (final RuntimeException closeTaskException) {\n790:                 final String uncleanMessage = String.format(\n791:                     \"Failed to close task %s cleanly. Attempting to close remaining tasks before re-throwing:\",\n792:                     task.id());\n793:                 log.error(uncleanMessage, closeTaskException);\n794: \n795:                 if (task.state() != State.CLOSED) {\n796:                     tasksToCloseDirty.add(task);\n797:                 }\n798: \n799:                 taskCloseExceptions.putIfAbsent(task.id(), closeTaskException);\n800:             }\n801:         }\n802: \n803:         tasksToRecycle.keySet().removeAll(tasksToCloseDirty);\n804:         for (final Map.Entry<Task, Set<TopicPartition>> entry : tasksToRecycle.entrySet()) {\n805:             final Task oldTask = entry.getKey();\n806:             final Set<TopicPartition> inputPartitions = entry.getValue();\n807:             try {\n808:                 if (oldTask.isActive()) {\n809:                     final StandbyTask standbyTask = convertActiveToStandby((StreamTask) oldTask, inputPartitions);\n810:                     if (stateUpdater != null) {\n811:                         tasks.removeTask(oldTask);\n812:                         tasks.addPendingTasksToInit(Collections.singleton(standbyTask));\n813:                     } else {\n814:                         tasks.replaceActiveWithStandby(standbyTask);\n815:                     }\n816:                 } else {\n817:                     final StreamTask activeTask = convertStandbyToActive((StandbyTask) oldTask, inputPartitions);\n818:                     tasks.replaceStandbyWithActive(activeTask);\n819:                 }\n820:             } catch (final RuntimeException e) {\n821:                 final String uncleanMessage = String.format(\"Failed to recycle task %s cleanly. \" +\n822:                     \"Attempting to close remaining tasks before re-throwing:\", oldTask.id());\n823:                 log.error(uncleanMessage, e);\n824:                 taskCloseExceptions.putIfAbsent(oldTask.id(), e);\n825:                 tasksToCloseDirty.add(oldTask);\n826:             }\n827:         }\n828: \n829:         // for tasks that cannot be cleanly closed or recycled, close them dirty\n830:         for (final Task task : tasksToCloseDirty) {\n831:             closeTaskDirty(task, true);\n832:         }\n833: \n834:         return taskCloseExceptions;\n835:     }\n836: \n837:     private StandbyTask convertActiveToStandby(final StreamTask activeTask, final Set<TopicPartition> partitions) {\n838:         return standbyTaskCreator.createStandbyTaskFromActive(activeTask, partitions);\n839:     }\n840: \n841:     private StreamTask convertStandbyToActive(final StandbyTask standbyTask, final Set<TopicPartition> partitions) {\n842:         return activeTaskCreator.createActiveTaskFromStandby(standbyTask, partitions, mainConsumer);\n843:     }\n844: \n845:     /**\n846:      * Tries to initialize any new or still-uninitialized tasks, then checks if they can/have completed restoration.\n847:      *\n848:      * @throws IllegalStateException If store gets registered after initialized is already finished\n849:      * @throws StreamsException if the store's change log does not contain the partition\n850:      * @return {@code true} if all tasks are fully restored\n851:      */\n852:     boolean tryToCompleteRestoration(final long now,\n853:                                      final java.util.function.Consumer<Set<TopicPartition>> offsetResetter) {\n854:         boolean allRunning = true;\n855: \n856:         // transit to restore active is idempotent so we can call it multiple times\n857:         changelogReader.enforceRestoreActive();\n858: \n859:         final List<Task> activeTasks = new LinkedList<>();\n860:         for (final Task task : tasks.allTasks()) {\n861:             try {\n862:                 task.initializeIfNeeded();\n863:                 task.clearTaskTimeout();\n864:             } catch (final LockException lockException) {\n865:                 // it is possible that if there are multiple threads within the instance that one thread\n866:                 // trying to grab the task from the other, while the other has not released the lock since\n867:                 // it did not participate in the rebalance. In this case we can just retry in the next iteration\n868:                 log.debug(\"Could not initialize task {} since: {}; will retry\", task.id(), lockException.getMessage());\n869:                 allRunning = false;\n870:             } catch (final TimeoutException timeoutException) {\n871:                 task.maybeInitTaskTimeoutOrThrow(now, timeoutException);\n872:                 allRunning = false;\n873:             }\n874: \n875:             if (task.isActive()) {\n876:                 activeTasks.add(task);\n877:             }\n878:         }\n879: \n880:         if (allRunning && !activeTasks.isEmpty()) {\n881: \n882:             final Set<TopicPartition> restored = changelogReader.completedChangelogs();\n883: \n884:             for (final Task task : activeTasks) {\n885:                 if (restored.containsAll(task.changelogPartitions())) {\n886:                     try {\n887:                         task.completeRestoration(offsetResetter);\n888:                         task.clearTaskTimeout();\n889:                     } catch (final TimeoutException timeoutException) {\n890:                         task.maybeInitTaskTimeoutOrThrow(now, timeoutException);\n891:                         log.debug(\n892:                             String.format(\n893:                                 \"Could not complete restoration for %s due to the following exception; will retry\",\n894:                                 task.id()),\n895:                             timeoutException\n896:                         );\n897: \n898:                         allRunning = false;\n899:                     }\n900:                 } else {\n901:                     // we found a restoring task that isn't done restoring, which is evidence that\n902:                     // not all tasks are running\n903:                     allRunning = false;\n904:                 }\n905:             }\n906:         }\n907:         if (allRunning) {\n908:             // we can call resume multiple times since it is idempotent.\n909:             mainConsumer.resume(mainConsumer.assignment());\n910:             changelogReader.transitToUpdateStandby();\n911:         }\n912: \n913:         return allRunning;\n914:     }\n915: \n916:     public boolean checkStateUpdater(final long now,\n917:                                      final java.util.function.Consumer<Set<TopicPartition>> offsetResetter) {\n918:         addTasksToStateUpdater();\n919:         if (stateUpdater.hasExceptionsAndFailedTasks()) {\n920:             handleExceptionsFromStateUpdater();\n921:         }\n922:         if (stateUpdater.restoresActiveTasks()) {\n923:             handleRestoredTasksFromStateUpdater(now, offsetResetter);\n924:         }\n925:         return !stateUpdater.restoresActiveTasks()\n926:             && !tasks.hasPendingTasksToInit();\n927:     }\n928: \n929:     private void recycleTaskFromStateUpdater(final Task task,\n930:                                              final Set<TopicPartition> inputPartitions,\n931:                                              final Set<Task> tasksToCloseDirty,\n932:                                              final Map<TaskId, RuntimeException> taskExceptions) {\n933:         Task newTask = null;\n934:         try {\n935:             task.suspend();\n936:             newTask = task.isActive() ?\n937:                 convertActiveToStandby((StreamTask) task, inputPartitions) :\n938:                 convertStandbyToActive((StandbyTask) task, inputPartitions);\n939:             tasks.addPendingTasksToInit(Collections.singleton(newTask));\n940:         } catch (final RuntimeException e) {\n941:             final TaskId taskId = task.id();\n942:             final String uncleanMessage = String.format(\"Failed to recycle task %s cleanly. \" +\n943:                 \"Attempting to close remaining tasks before re-throwing:\", taskId);\n944:             log.error(uncleanMessage, e);\n945: \n946:             if (task.state() != State.CLOSED) {\n947:                 tasksToCloseDirty.add(task);\n948:             }\n949:             if (newTask != null && newTask.state() != State.CLOSED) {\n950:                 tasksToCloseDirty.add(newTask);\n951:             }\n952: \n953:             taskExceptions.putIfAbsent(taskId, e);\n954:         }\n955:     }\n956: \n957:     private void closeTaskClean(final Task task,\n958:                                 final Set<Task> tasksToCloseDirty,\n959:                                 final Map<TaskId, RuntimeException> taskExceptions) {\n960:         try {\n961:             task.suspend();\n962:             task.closeClean();\n963:         } catch (final RuntimeException e) {\n964:             final String uncleanMessage = String.format(\"Failed to close task %s cleanly. \" +\n965:                 \"Attempting to close remaining tasks before re-throwing:\", task.id());\n966:             log.error(uncleanMessage, e);\n967: \n968:             if (task.state() != State.CLOSED) {\n969:                 tasksToCloseDirty.add(task);\n970:             }\n971: \n972:             taskExceptions.putIfAbsent(task.id(), e);\n973:         }\n974:     }\n975: \n976:     private void transitRestoredTaskToRunning(final Task task,\n977:                                               final long now,\n978:                                               final java.util.function.Consumer<Set<TopicPartition>> offsetResetter) {\n979:         try {\n980:             task.completeRestoration(offsetResetter);\n981:             tasks.addTask(task);\n982:             mainConsumer.resume(task.inputPartitions());\n983:             task.clearTaskTimeout();\n984:         } catch (final TimeoutException timeoutException) {\n985:             task.maybeInitTaskTimeoutOrThrow(now, timeoutException);\n986:             stateUpdater.add(task);\n987:             log.debug(\n988:                 String.format(\n989:                     \"Could not complete restoration for %s due to the following exception; adding the task \" +\n990:                         \"back to the state updater and will retry\",\n991:                     task.id()),\n992:                 timeoutException\n993:             );\n994:         }\n995:     }\n996: \n997:     private void addTasksToStateUpdater() {\n998:         final Map<TaskId, RuntimeException> taskExceptions = new LinkedHashMap<>();\n999:         for (final Task task : tasks.drainPendingTasksToInit()) {\n1000:             try {\n1001:                 addTaskToStateUpdater(task);\n1002:             } catch (final RuntimeException e) {\n1003:                 // need to add task back to the bookkeeping to be handled by the stream thread\n1004:                 tasks.addFailedTask(task);\n1005:                 taskExceptions.put(task.id(), e);\n1006:             }\n1007:         }\n1008: \n1009:         maybeThrowTaskExceptions(taskExceptions);\n1010:     }\n1011: \n1012:     private void addTaskToStateUpdater(final Task task) {\n1013:         final long nowMs = time.milliseconds();\n1014:         try {\n1015:             if (canTryInitializeTask(task.id(), nowMs)) {\n1016:                 task.initializeIfNeeded();\n1017:                 taskIdToBackoffRecord.remove(task.id());\n1018:                 stateUpdater.add(task);\n1019:             } else {\n1020:                 log.trace(\"Task {} is still not allowed to retry acquiring the state directory lock\", task.id());\n1021:                 tasks.addPendingTasksToInit(Collections.singleton(task));\n1022:             }\n1023:         } catch (final LockException lockException) {\n1024:             // The state directory may still be locked by another thread, when the rebalance just happened.\n1025:             // Retry in the next iteration.\n1026:             log.info(\"Encountered lock exception. Reattempting locking the state in the next iteration. Error message was: {}\",\n1027:                      lockException.getMessage());\n1028:             tasks.addPendingTasksToInit(Collections.singleton(task));\n1029:             updateOrCreateBackoffRecord(task.id(), nowMs);\n1030:         }\n1031:     }\n1032: \n1033:     public void handleExceptionsFromStateUpdater() {\n1034:         final Map<TaskId, RuntimeException> taskExceptions = collectExceptionsAndFailedTasksFromStateUpdater();\n1035:         maybeThrowTaskExceptions(taskExceptions);\n1036:     }\n1037: \n1038:     public Map<TaskId, RuntimeException> collectExceptionsAndFailedTasksFromStateUpdater() {\n1039:         final Map<TaskId, RuntimeException> taskExceptions = new LinkedHashMap<>();\n1040: \n1041:         for (final final Task failedTask = exceptionAndTask.task();\n1044:             // need to add task back to the bookkeeping to be handled by the stream thread\n1045: private void handleRestoredTasksFromStateUpdater(final long now,\n1053:                                                      final java.util.function.Consumer<Set<TopicPartition>> offsetResetter) {\n1054:         final Duration timeout = Duration.ZERO;\n1055:         for (final Task task : stateUpdater.drainRestoredActiveTasks(timeout)) {\n1056:             transitRestoredTaskToRunning(task, now, offsetResetter);\n1057:         }\n1058:     }\n1059: \n1060:     /**\n1061:      * Handle the revoked partitions and prepare for closing the associated tasks in {@link #handleAssignment(Map, Map)}\n1062:      * We should commit the revoking tasks first before suspending them as we will not officially own them anymore when\n1063:      * {@link #handleAssignment(Map, Map)} is called. Note that only active task partitions are passed in from the\n1064:      * rebalance listener, so we only need to consider/commit active tasks here\n1065:      *\n1066:      * If eos-v2 is used, we must commit ALL tasks. Otherwise, we can just commit those (active) tasks which are revoked\n1067:      *\n1068:      * @throws TaskMigratedException if the task producer got fenced (EOS only)\n1069:      */\n1070:     void handleRevocation(final Collection<TopicPartition> revokedPartitions) {\n1071:         final Set<TopicPartition> remainingRevokedPartitions = new HashSet<>(revokedPartitions);\n1072: \n1073:         final Set<Task> revokedActiveTasks = new HashSet<>();\n1074:         final Set<Task> commitNeededActiveTasks = new HashSet<>();\n1075:         final Map<Task, Map<TopicPartition, OffsetAndMetadata>> consumedOffsetsPerTask = new HashMap<>();\n1076:         final AtomicReference<RuntimeException> firstException = new AtomicReference<>(null);\n1077: \n1078:         final Set<TaskId> lockedTaskIds = activeRunningTaskIterable().stream().map(Task::id).collect(Collectors.toSet());\n1079:         maybeLockTasks(lockedTaskIds);\n1080: \n1081:         boolean revokedTasksNeedCommit = false;\n1082:         for (final Task task : activeRunningTaskIterable()) {\n1083:             if (remainingRevokedPartitions.containsAll(task.inputPartitions())) {\n1084:                 // when the task input partitions are included in the revoked list,\n1085:                 // this is an active task and should be revoked\n1086: \n1087:                 revokedActiveTasks.add(task);\n1088:                 remainingRevokedPartitions.removeAll(task.inputPartitions());\n1089: \n1090:                 revokedTasksNeedCommit |= task.commitNeeded();\n1091:             } else if (task.commitNeeded()) {\n1092:                 commitNeededActiveTasks.add(task);\n1093:             }\n1094:         }\n1095: \n1096:         revokeTasksInStateUpdater(remainingRevokedPartitions);\n1097: \n1098:         if (!remainingRevokedPartitions.isEmpty()) {\n1099:             log.debug(\"The following revoked partitions {} are missing from the current task partitions. It could \"\n1100:                           + \"potentially be due to race condition of consumer detecting the heartbeat failure, or the tasks \" +\n1101:                          \"have been cleaned up by the handleAssignment callback.\", remainingRevokedPartitions);\n1102:         }\n1103: \n1104:         if (revokedTasksNeedCommit) {\n1105:             prepareCommitAndAddOffsetsToMap(revokedActiveTasks, consumedOffsetsPerTask);\n1106:             // if we need to commit any revoking task then we just commit all of those needed committing together\n1107:             prepareCommitAndAddOffsetsToMap(commitNeededActiveTasks, consumedOffsetsPerTask);\n1108:         }\n1109: \n1110:         // even if commit failed, we should still continue and complete suspending those tasks, so we would capture\n1111:         // any exception and rethrow it at the end. some exceptions may be handled immediately and then swallowed,\n1112:         // as such we just need to skip those dirty tasks in the checkpoint\n1113:         final Set<Task> dirtyTasks = new HashSet<>();\n1114:         try {\n1115:             if (revokedTasksNeedCommit) {\n1116:                 // in handleRevocation we must call commitOffsetsOrTransaction() directly rather than\n1117:                 // commitAndFillInConsumedOffsetsAndMetadataPerTaskMap() to make sure we don't skip the\n1118:                 // offset commit because we are in a rebalance\n1119:                 taskExecutor.commitOffsetsOrTransaction(consumedOffsetsPerTask);\n1120:             }\n1121:         } catch (final TaskCorruptedException e) {\n1122:             log.warn(\"Some tasks were corrupted when trying to commit offsets, these will be cleaned and revived: {}\",\n1123:                      e.corruptedTasks());\n1124: \n1125:             // If we hit a TaskCorruptedException it must be EOS, just handle the cleanup for those corrupted tasks right here\n1126:             dirtyTasks.addAll(tasks.tasks(e.corruptedTasks()));\n1127:             closeDirtyAndRevive(dirtyTasks, true);\n1128:         } catch (final TimeoutException e) {\n1129:             log.warn(\"Timed out while trying to commit all tasks during revocation, these will be cleaned and revived\");\n1130: \n1131:             // If we hit a TimeoutException it must be ALOS, just close dirty and revive without wiping the state\n1132:             dirtyTasks.addAll(consumedOffsetsPerTask.keySet());\n1133:             closeDirtyAndRevive(dirtyTasks, false);\n1134:         } catch (final RuntimeException e) {\n1135:             log.error(\"Exception caught while committing those revoked tasks \" + revokedActiveTasks, e);\n1136:             firstException.compareAndSet(null, e);\n1137:             dirtyTasks.addAll(consumedOffsetsPerTask.keySet());\n1138:         }\n1139: \n1140:         // we enforce checkpointing upon suspending a task: if it is resumed later we just proceed normally, if it is\n1141:         // going to be closed we would checkpoint by then\n1142:         for (final Task task : revokedActiveTasks) {\n1143:             if (!dirtyTasks.contains(task)) {\n1144:                 try {\n1145:                     task.postCommit(true);\n1146:                 } catch (final RuntimeException e) {\n1147:                     log.error(\"Exception caught while post-committing task \" + task.id(), e);\n1148:                     maybeSetFirstException(false, maybeWrapTaskException(e, task.id()), firstException);\n1149:                 }\n1150:             }\n1151:         }\n1152: \n1153:         if (revokedTasksNeedCommit) {\n1154:             for (final Task task : commitNeededActiveTasks) {\n1155:                 if (!dirtyTasks.contains(task)) {\n1156:                     try {\n1157:                         // for non-revoking active tasks, we should not enforce checkpoint\n1158:                         // since if it is EOS enabled, no checkpoint should be written while\n1159:                         // the task is in RUNNING tate\n1160:                         task.postCommit(false);\n1161:                     } catch (final RuntimeException e) {\n1162:                         log.error(\"Exception caught while post-committing task \" + task.id(), e);\n1163:                         maybeSetFirstException(false, maybeWrapTaskException(e, task.id()), firstException);\n1164:                     }\n1165:                 }\n1166:             }\n1167:         }\n1168: \n1169:         for (final Task task : revokedActiveTasks) {\n1170:             try {\n1171:                 task.suspend();\n1172:             } catch (final RuntimeException e) {\n1173:                 log.error(\"Caught the following exception while trying to suspend revoked task \" + task.id(), e);\n1174:                 maybeSetFirstException(false, maybeWrapTaskException(e, task.id()), firstException);\n1175:             }\n1176:         }\n1177: \n1178:         maybeUnlockTasks(lockedTaskIds);\n1179: \n1180:         if (firstException.get() != null) {\n1181:             throw firstException.get();\n1182:         }\n1183:     }\n1184: \n1185:     private void revokeTasksInStateUpdater(final Set<TopicPartition> remainingRevokedPartitions) {\n1186:         if (stateUpdater != null) {\n1187:             final HashMap<>();\n1189:             for (final Task restoringTask : stateUpdater.tasks()) {\n1190:                 if (restoringTask.isActive()) {\n1191:                     if            remainingRevokedPartitions.removeAll(restoringTask.inputPartitions());\n1194:                     }\n1195:                 }\n1196:             }\n1197:       maybeThrowTaskExceptions(failedTasksFromStateUpdater);\n1203:         }\n1204:     }\n1205: \n1206:     private void prepareCommitAndAddOffsetsToMap(final Set<Task> tasksToPrepare,\n1207:                                                  final Map<Task, Map<TopicPartition, OffsetAndMetadata>> consumedOffsetsPerTask) {\n1208:         for (final Task task : tasksToPrepare) {\n1209:    /**\n1224:      * Closes active tasks as zombies, as these partitions have been lost and are no longer owned.\n1225:      * NOTE this method assumes that when it is called, EVERY task/partition has Tasks\n1329:      * that we locked but didn't own will be released at the end of the rebalance (unless of course we were\n1330:      * assigned the task as a result of the rebalance). This method should be idempotent.\n1331:      */\n1332:     private void tryToLockAllNonEmptyTaskDirectories() {\n1333:         // Always clear the set at the beginning as we're always dealing with the\n1334:         // current set of actually-locked tasks.\n1335:         lockedTaskDirectories.clear();\n1336: \n1337:         final Map<TaskId, Task> allTasks = allTasks();\n1338:         for               taskIdIterator.remove();\n1372:             }\n1373:         }\n1374:     }\n1375: \n1376:     /**\n1377:      * We must release the lock for any unassigned tasks that we temporarily {\n1393:         long offsetSum = 0L;\n1394:         for (final Map.Entry<TopicPartition, Long> changelogEntry : changelogOffsets.entrySet()) {\n1395:             final long offset =           final AtomicReference<RuntimeException> firstException) {\n1563:         if (!clean) {\n1564:             return activeTaskIterable();\n1565:         }\n1566:         final Comparator<Task>                                  final Runnable runnable,\n2127:                                               final java.util.function.Consumer<RuntimeException> actionIfClean,\n2128:
